# SEG Phase 7.8.7 — Legacy Object Crosswalk

## Scope and decision discipline

This is an object-level evidence crosswalk only. It is not a production record set, import payload, medical review, rights decision, or approval artifact. Each row is derived from the deterministic crosswalk TSV and preserves observed identifiers/labels only. Missing candidate joins remain `UNRESOLVED`; no medical fields, versions, rights, citations, or approvals are inferred.

## Evidence basis

- Legacy object rows analyzed: **1,919** across the eight supplied JSON assets.
- SEG candidate rows available for comparison: **48** from the existing candidate map.
- Matching rule: exact observed identity/key or exact observed title/topic/path-stem equality only; no fuzzy matching.
- Crosswalk summary SHA-256: `f28f368afe15463a499f5390b34fc682d901357c55acf8769d97f2d1f3dcae34`.
- All eight source asset SHA-256 values are listed in the appendix.
